# Step preview prompts

Generation: built-in image_gen. PNG masters and matching JPEG exports are 1080 x 1920.
Retained and shared assets list their source image prompt; regenerated assets list the actual new prompt.

## Mandatory constraints for future generation and reuse

Visitor-accessible camera viewpoint only: place the camera in an ordinary customer area. Never place the camera behind a staff counter or till, inside a working kitchen, at a staff-only prep bench, or in another restricted area. Show staff activity from the customer side or through a public viewing window. Do not assume special access. Adapt any conflicting shot direction to a visitor-side view, using a served item or customer-facing hand-off if the work is not visible from a public area. Close and top-down framing must remain physically possible without crossing the customer boundary.

At most two visible hands total in the entire image, counted across all people, including partial and background hands. Zero or one hand is fine. Keep hands anatomically plausible and attached to their owners. No extra, duplicated or detached hands. Frame all other hands out of the composition.

When makeup or cosmetic application is shown, draw it more schematically: simple face contours, minimal eye, lip and brow marks, flat accent areas for cosmetics, and a recognizable brush or applicator. Keep the application action clear at thumbnail size. Avoid realistic skin texture, individual lashes, detailed eyes and glossy beauty-ad shading. Do not reduce the subject to generic geometric symbols.

Historical prompts below document existing artwork; they are not exemptions from these constraints. Append all constraints and adapt any conflicting direction before regenerating. See viewpoint-audit.md for existing images flagged by the targeted review.

## bakery-fresh-batch_oven.png - Out of the oven

Faces simplified (no detailed facial features), people of varied ages and backgrounds. Scene: a bakery, behind the counter. Medium shot, one person framed from the waist up. Action: Trays coming out of the oven.
